# Task 1.
A car passed the distance s between the city A to B with velocity v$_1$ = 50 km/h, and
back with velocity v$_{2}$ = 40 km/h. Calculate the average velocity.

$\text{Average velocity}=\frac{\text{total displacement​}}{\text{total time}}$

ANS: Since the car started and ended at the same position its average velocity is 0

# Task 2.
A car passed a certain distance s from the city A do B with velocity v$_{1}$. With what
velocity v$_{2}$ should the car move back to obtain the average velocity equal to 2v$_{1}$?

$\text{Average velocity}=\frac{\text{total displacement​}}{\text{total time}}$

ANS: If the car starts and ends in the same position its average velocity will always be 0

# Task 3.
The ship traveled 2 km to the east, then 5 km to the north, and then 5 km to a direction
deviating 60 degrees from the north to the east. Each section of the travel was covered in 1
hour. Draw the scheme of the ship's travel using vectors. Find the ship's displacement vector
from its starting point. Determine the value of the path traveled by the ship, the length of the
displacement vector and the average velocity vector

The ship traveled in total 6.33km to the east and 7.5km to the north
the total displacement is $d=\sqrt{ 6.33^{2}+7.5^{2} }=9.81km$

$v_{avg}=\frac{9.81km}{3h}=3.27km/h$

# Task 4.
The rain drops fall down vertically with a constant velocity v1 = 70 m/s. Describe this
motion with respect of the train moving horizontally with velocity v2 = 30 m/s.

$v_{x}=\sqrt{ 70^{2}+30^{2} }\approx76.1577m/s$
$\alpha=\arctan\left( \frac{70}{30} \right)\approx 66.8\degree$

# task 5
Two ships started to move simultaneously in the perpendicular directions with
velocities v1 = 20 km/h and v2 = 30 km/h, respectively. Calculate the velocity of the mutual
distancing vx of the ships and their distance d after time t = 4 h.

316.6689076245232) rotate(0 22.128150939941406 7.5)"><text x="0" y="10.572" font-family="Excalifont, Xiaolai, sans-serif, Segoe UI Emoji" font-size="12px" fill="#1e1e1e" text-anchor="start" style="white-space: pre;" direction="ltr" dominant-baseline="alphabetic">20km/h</text></g><g stroke-linecap="round"><g transform="translate(68.32884095229247 11.24896572895068) rotate(0 sans-serif, Segoe UI Emoji" font-size="12px" fill="#2f9e44" text-anchor="start" style="white-space: pre;" direction="ltr" dominant-baseline="alphabetic">36km/h</text></g></svg>
distancing /h = $\sqrt{ 30^{2}+20^{2} }\approx 36km/h$
after 4 hours $\sqrt{ (30·4)^{2}+(20·4)^{2} }\approx 144.222km$

# task 6
A ship travels upstream of the river with a constant velocity v1 = 3.8 km/h, and down
the river with velocity v2 = 6.2 km/h. Determine the ship velocity v3 in the water at rest and
the river velocity v4. 


ship moving upstream $v_{3}-v_{4}=3.8km/h$
ship moving downstream $v_{3}+v_{4}=6.2km/h$

$v_{3}-v_{4}+v_{3}+v_{4}=3.8+6.2$
$2v_{3}=10km/h$
$v_{3}=5km/h$

$5km/h+v_{4}=6.2km/h$
$v_{4}=1.2km/h$

ANS: ship in still water $v_{3}=5km/h$, river velocity $v_{4}=1.2km/h$

# task 7
The ball is rolling in a train carriage perpendicularly to the direction of the train
motion, with velocity v1 = 8 m/s (with respect to the carriage). The train travels with velocity
v2 = 60 km/h. What is the value v and direction of the ball velocity with respect to the Earth?

y="25.374355660826822" font-family="Excalifont, Xiaolai, sans-serif, Segoe UI Emoji" font-size="11.907252773733843px" fill="#1e1e1e" text-anchor="start" style="white-space: pre;" direction="ltr" dominant-baseline="alphabetic">25.64°</text></g></svg>
$v_{1}=8m/s$
$v_{2}=60km/h\approx 16.66m/s$
$v_{3}=\sqrt{ 8^{2}+16.66^{2} }\approx 18.48m/s$
$\alpha=\arctan\left( \frac{8}{16.67} \right)\approx 25.64\degree$
# task 8
The train, which travels with velocity v1 = 80 km/h was penetrated by a ball fired
perpendicularly to its trajectory with velocity v2 = 200 m/s. What is the value v and direction
of the ball velocity with respect to the reference frame connected with the train?
![[Pasted image 20260829134139.png]]
$v_{1}=80km/h\approx 22.22m/s$
$v_{2}=200m/s$
$v_{3}=\sqrt{ 22.22^{2}+200^{2} }\approx201.2m/s$
$\alpha=\arctan\left( \frac{22.22}{200} \right)\approx 6.34\degree$ + 90°
the ball is traveling at 96.34° relative to train
# task 9
What should be the airship rudder direction (the own velocity v1 = 100 km/h), and the
value of the airship net velocity v directed from the south to north if the wind blows from the
northern-east to southern-west with velocity v2 = 8 m/s.

its speed relative to ground will be reduced to 21.5m/s

# task 10

A boat travels in the perpendicular direction to the river current. The own velocity
of the boat is v = 1.8 m/s and the river width is l = 54 m. Due to the water flow the boat
landed at a distance s = 15 m below the starting point (measured along the river bank). What
is the water velocity v1?

$t=\frac{54m/s}{1.8m}=30s$
$v=\frac{15m}{30s}=0.5m/s$

ANS: The boat took 30 seconds to travel across the river and the river pushed it down at 0.5m/s resulting in boat landing 15m lower

# task 11
A ship moves with uniform rectilinear motion with velocity v = 25 km/h. A motor
boat moves towards the ship in the perpendicular direction with velocity v1 = 40 km/h. What
is the boat motion (v2) with respect to the observer at the ship? 

$v_{2}=\sqrt{ 25^{2}+40^{2} }\approx47.16m/s$
$\alpha=\arctan\left( \frac{25}{40} \right)\approx 32\degree$ +90°
Ans: the boat is moving at 47.16km/h  and bearing 122° relative to ship
